### [1] [Decay and structure of heavy flavour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18098)
*Stefan Groote,Arpan Chatterjee,Maria Naeem*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了COST行动CA24159下塔尔图工作组在强子物理中的若干研究方向，涵盖粲重子产生与非轻子衰变的流代数方法、长程重散射导致的CP破坏、中子β衰变中新物理探针、极化W玻色子辐射修正、同种轻子四体Higgs衰变中的全同粒子与质量效应，以及基于非局域场算符的内禀粲机制解释（调和SELEX与LHCb分歧），并结合非局域NJL模型与相对论Faddeev方法构建强子态描述框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为促进COST行动CA24159各参与组间交流，系统梳理塔尔图工作组在强子结构与谱学中的核心研究议题，尤其聚焦于理论方法的统一性与实验反常（如SELEX/LHCb差异）的物理解释。

Method: 采用流代数结合张量不变量分析粲重子非轻子衰变；引入非局域场算符与非局域Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型，并耦合相对论Faddeev方程求解强子束缚态；运用螺旋度形式论计算电弱辐射修正及四体末态相空间中的全同粒子对称性效应。

Result: 建立了基于流代数的粲重子衰变振幅参数化方案，可分离短距与长距CP破坏贡献；提出非局域算符是解释内禀粲机制的关键，成功调和SELEX与LHCb在粲重子产生截面上的分歧；发展了适用于极化W衰变与H→4ℓ（同味）的螺旋度振幅框架，显式包含质量与全同粒子效应；非局域NJL+Faddeev框架可自洽生成类核子与类Δ强子态。

Conclusion: 塔尔图工作组的研究强调理论工具（流代数、非局域有效场论、Faddeev方法、螺旋度形式论）在连接QCD第一性原理与强子现象学之间的桥梁作用；多个方向指向长程动力学（重散射、非局域性、末态相互作用）对精密强子物理观测的关键影响；若干开放问题涉及非局域尺度的确定、内禀粲的格点检验、以及多体末态中QCD与EW混合效应的系统刻画。

Abstract: In accordance to the aim of the constituing meeting of the COST action CA24159 ``Structure and Spectroscopy of Hadrons Research Project'' to introduce the different groups to the action, in this talk we give an overview over the subjects dealt with by the working group in Tartu related to hadron physics. We deal with the production and the nonleptonic decays of charmed baryons in the framework of the current algebra approach in terms of tensor invariants and explain how this approach can be used to approach CP violation via long distance effects in rescattering. New physics effects can be even seen in the classical neutron beta decay, but the helicity approach used here is also useful for e.g.\ calculating first order electroweak radiative corrections to the decay of the polarised $W$ boson. Identical particle and mass effects are seen in the Higgs decay into four leptons of the same type. The second main part starts with indications for the intrinsic charm mechanism, explaining the discrepancy between the results of SELEX and LHCb. The solution offered here is valid only if one considers nonlocal field operators. The nonlocal extension of the Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model, derived directly from QCD and combined with the relativistic Faddeev approach, allows for the description of hadronic states. We conclude by presenting open questions to the action.

</details>


### [2] [Phasing out Dark Matter Isocurvature with Thermal Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18132)
*Brian Batell,Akshay Ghalsasi,Subhajit Ghosh,Mudit Rai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出热错位机制作为标量暗物质宇宙学产生的新途径，通过有限温度势驱动标量场在辐射主导时期早期获得大场值，从而动态诱导错位；该机制能自然抑制由暴胀涨落引发的暗物质等熵扰动，规避CMB对轻标量场的强约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准错位机制中轻标量暗物质因暴胀量子涨落导致强等熵扰动、与CMB观测严重冲突的问题。

Method: 构建含微弱热浴耦合的标量场有限温度有效势模型，分析其在辐射主导时期的动力学演化及超视界扰动的相位演化，计算最终暗物质密度对比度与等熵扰动功率谱。

Result: 热错位机制可产生显著的晚时背景零模与超视界扰动之间的相位偏移，压低最终暗物质密度对比度，从而大幅削弱等熵扰动信号。

Conclusion: 热错位是一种普适且免于CMB等熵约束的标量暗物质产生机制，拓展了轻标量暗物质的可行参数空间。

Abstract: Thermal misalignment provides an alternative to the standard misalignment mechanism for the cosmological production of scalar dark matter. In this framework, feeble couplings to particles in the thermal bath generate a finite-temperature potential that drives the scalar towards large field values early in the radiation era, dynamically inducing the misalignment before the onset of scalar oscillations. As a result, the relic abundance is controlled primarily by particle masses and couplings rather than the initial field value. As a light spectator field, the scalar acquires inflationary fluctuations that are uncorrelated with the adiabatic curvature mode, generically sourcing isocurvature perturbations. We show that, unlike standard misalignment, where light scalars are strongly constrained by cosmic microwave background bounds on dark matter isocurvature for high-scale inflation, thermal misalignment can naturally suppress the isocurvature signal. This occurs through a novel late-time phase offset between the background zero mode and the superhorizon perturbations, which reduces the final dark matter density contrast. Thermal misalignment therefore provides a new and generic route to isocurvature-safe scalar dark matter.

</details>


### [3] [Vector Resonances at Muon and Wakefield Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18146)
*Massimo Cipressi,Kevin Langhoff,Toby Opferkuch*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来高能轻子对撞机（特别是基于等离子体尾场加速的 wakefield colliders）探测重矢量共振态（如动力学混合的 Z'）的能力；指出传统上被视为不利因素的束流辐射（beamstrahlung）反而通过展宽质心能量谱，实现了对共振质量的大范围扫描，从而显著提升探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重矢量共振态（如 Z'）是超出标准模型物理的重要探针，但其质量若远高于对撞机标称质心能量，则难以在固定能量对撞机中直接产生；需发展新策略提升探测能力。

Method: 定量分析 wakefield 对撞机中 beamstrahlung 引起的质心能量谱展宽效应，结合共振截面的 Breit–Wigner 形状，计算有效积分亮度对共振质量的覆盖能力，并给出 Z' 探测灵敏度的参数空间投影。

Result: beamstrahlung 显著拓宽了有效质心能量扫描范围，使 wakefield 对撞机对质量高达数 TeV 的 Z' 共振态仍具可观探测灵敏度，优于同等标称能量的传统线性对撞机。

Conclusion: beamstrahlung 在 wakefield 对撞机中并非纯噪声源，而是可被系统利用的物理资源，为重共振态搜索提供了新型‘能量扫描范式’，凸显了新型加速技术在高能物理探索中的独特优势。

Abstract: We explore the potential of future high-energy lepton colliders to probe heavy vector resonances. At wakefield colliders, intense beam-beam interactions produce radiation, called beamstrahlung, which redistributes luminosity from the nominal energy across a broad spectrum of lower collision energies. We show that this effect, conventionally viewed as a drawback, dramatically enhances sensitivity to resonances by effectively scanning a wide range of center-of-mass energies. We present projections for a benchmark scenario of a heavy kinetically mixed $Z'$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索中子星内部极端致密状态下量子色动力学（QCD）非微扰行为，特别是夸克物质相变的可观测天体物理信号。

Method: 基于致密星体物态方程与相变动力学模型，结合广义相对论线性扰动理论，估算夸克核形成过程中的非轴对称质量四极矩变化所激发的MHz频段引力波辐射特征。

Result: 预言超新星坍缩晚期夸克核快速形成阶段可产生持续数毫秒、中心频率约0.1–10 MHz、应变幅度h ~ 10^{-23}–10^{-21}的引力波瞬变信号。

Conclusion: MHz频段引力波是探测中子星内夸克物质相变的潜在新信使，有望通过未来高灵敏度高频探测器（如DOCOMO、GEO-HF）验证QCD在数倍核密度下的手征恢复与去禁闭特性。

Abstract: Tentative evidence suggests that the cores of massive neutron stars consist of deconfined quark matter. We argue that the formation of such a quark matter core during a galactic supernova could be accompanied by the emission of gravitational waves in the MHz band. These signals constitute a new target for high-frequency gravitational wave detectors, demonstrating that such detectors may offer unique opportunities for testing quantum chromodynamics in an otherwise inaccessible regime.

</details>


### [5] [Two-Component Dark Matter in the Type-I 2HDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18158)
*Patricio Escalona,Jacinto P. Neto,M. J. Neves,Camila Ramos,David Suarez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了I型双Higgs二重态模型中由实标量s和Dirac费米子χ构成的双组分暗物质模型，基于Z₄对称性与运动学条件确保其稳定性；通过Higgs-portal与Yukawa相互作用耦合到可见扇区；系统分析热冻结机制下的湮灭、转化与半湮灭过程；在满足理论自洽（微扰性、真空稳定性）前提下进行多参数扫描，并结合宇宙学（Planck）、Higgs不可见衰变、直接探测（XENONnT等）及电弱精度观测量等实验约束；发现虽存在满足全部暗物质约束的参数空间，但LHC等对标量扇区的限制显著压缩可行区，尤其在亚TeV质量区间与暗物质唯象偏好产生张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单组分暗物质模型难以同时满足多种实验约束的问题，探索双组分机制如何缓解如直接探测过强、Higgs不可见分支比超标或热历史不兼容等张力，并检验扩展Higgs扇区（如2HDM）中暗物质的协同演化动力学。

Method: 构建具有Z₄对称性的I型2HDM暗物质模型，引入实标量s与Dirac费米子χ作为稳定暗物质候选者；建立包含s-s、χ-χ、s-χ间转换与半湮灭的完整热退耦输运方程组；采用Monte Carlo参数扫描，在物理质量、Higgs混合角、门户耦合等维度上施加理论边界（λ<4π, 有效势正定）与实验约束；数值求解Boltzmann方程并耦合MicrOMEGAs/CalcHEP进行截面计算。

Result: 识别出若干满足Ωh²=0.12±0.0012（Planck）、BR(h→inv)<0.11（ATLAS/CMS）、σ_SI<9.4×10⁻⁴⁷ cm²（XENONnT 2023）及S,T参数在1σ内的参数点；标量质量m_s∈[60,350] GeV与费米子质量m_χ∈[10,120] GeV区域仍部分存活，但m_s<125 GeV被h→ss衰变与LEP约束强烈压制；半湮灭过程χχs→ss对低质量区丰度调节起关键作用；Z₄对称性破缺尺度需高于TeV以避免NDA禁戒。

Conclusion: 双组分机制可在I型2HDM框架下协调多重暗物质约束，但LHC对标量Higgs-portal耦合的直接搜寻（如h→ss→4b/2γ2b）已对亚TeV标量暗物质构成决定性限制，暗示若该类模型成立，则暗物质主导成分更可能为费米子χ，且其质量应接近电弱尺度以维持正确丰度，而标量s则需极弱耦合或处于共振区（m_s≈m_h/2）以规避当前实验。

Abstract: We investigate a two-component dark matter scenario in the type-I two-Higgs-doublet model. The dark sector contains a real scalar $s$ and a Dirac fermion $χ$, whose stability is ensured by a $Z_4$ symmetry together with kinematic conditions. The scalar interacts with the visible sector through Higgs-portal couplings, while the fermion interacts with the scalar via Yukawa interactions. In this framework, we analyze the thermal freeze-out production of both candidates, accounting for annihilation, conversion, and semi-annihilation processes. A comprehensive scan over the multidimensional parameter space is performed in terms of physical masses, mixing angles, and portal couplings, imposing theoretical requirements such as perturbativity and vacuum stability. We confront the model with current experimental constraints, including the observed relic abundance, invisible Higgs decays, direct detection limits on spin-independent scattering cross sections, and electroweak precision observables. We find that viable regions of parameter space can satisfy all dark matter constraints, but collider bounds strongly constrain the scalar sector, narrowing the allowed regions and creating tension with those favored by dark matter phenomenology, particularly in the sub-TeV mass regime.

</details>

*Michele Cicoli,Francesco D'Eramo,Luca Di Luzio,Damiano F. G. Fiorillo,Maurizio Giannotti,Alicia Gomez,Diego Guadagnoli,Mathieu Kaltschmidt,Bradley J. Kavanagh,Alessandro Lella,Giuseppe Lucente,David J. E. Marsh,Federico Mescia,Alessandro Mirizzi,Javier Redondo,Nicole Righi,Jaime Ruz,Ken'ichi Saikawa,Elisa Todarello,Edoardo Vitagliano,Su-Yang Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了毫电子伏（meV）质量范围轴子的理论动机、宇宙学与天体物理效应，以及多种实验探测手段（如日光镜、晕镜、准粒子系统和大型切伦科夫探测器），强调该质量区间在下一代轴子发现中的核心地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: meV轴子在紫外完备模型中自然出现，可作为暗物质或暗辐射，且在恒星与超新星中高效产生，提供强天体物理约束；其质量尺度处于理论可解性、宇宙学相关性与实验可行性交汇点。

Method: 综述性研究，整合理论构造（PQ机制、弦论模型）、宇宙学演化（热历史、丰度计算）、天体物理探针（红巨星、SN1987A能量损失）、以及多类实验技术（helioscopes, haloscopes, magnon/phonon excitations, large-volume Cherenkov detectors）。

Result: 系统梳理了meV轴子参数空间当前约束（来自CAST、IAXO、ADMX、BREAD、BabyIAXO等）、未被覆盖的窗口、关键理论不确定性（如axion-photon耦合强度、初始场值、再加热温度依赖），以及跨学科协同探测的必要性。

Conclusion: meV轴子是连接粒子物理、宇宙学与天体物理的关键桥梁，多通道实验协同将在未来5–10年内决定该质量区间的可观测性；其发现将直接验证Peccei–Quinn机制并打开通往弦论低能有效理论的窗口。

Abstract: The meV mass range has emerged as a focal point in axion physics, where advances in theory, cosmology, astrophysics, and experimental techniques converge. Axions in this mass range are theoretically well motivated, can arise in ultraviolet-complete models, and can have significant cosmological impacts as dark matter or dark radiation. In parallel, their efficient production in stellar and supernova environments provides powerful astrophysical probes. Here, we provide a comprehensive overview of meV axions across these domains, highlighting both established results and open questions. We discuss the theoretical underpinnings of meV axions, their cosmological and astrophysical signatures, and the diverse experimental strategies -- ranging from helioscopes and haloscopes to quasiparticle systems and large-volume Cherenkov detectors -- that aim to explore this regime. The convergence of these approaches emphasizes the pivotal role of the meV mass range for axion discovery in the coming years, identifying meV axions as a key probe for testing beyond-Standard-Model physics. This review document is the direct outcome of the discussions at the dedicated workshop "The meV Mass Axion Frontier: Challenges and Opportunities", held at Laboratori Nazionali di Frascati (IT) on 27--28 October 2025, and organized by the EU funded COST Action "Cosmic WISPers in the Dark Universe: Theory, astrophysics, and experiments" (CA21106, https://www.cost.eu/actions/CA21106). Its aim is to provide an overview of current efforts in meV axion research, their motivations, and the research goals that animate the community involved in this search.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索洛伦兹对称性破缺背景下超对称粒子（尤其是光子/光微子）混合的新机制，弥补标准Primakoff效应在超对称框架下的缺失，并为LSV提供可观测天体物理探针。

Method: 采用超空间（superspace）与超场（superfield）形式化方法，将LSV参数嵌入非动力学超场；构造包含光子与光微子的动能混合矩阵；在恒星等离子体环境中计算由该混合导致的能量输运修正，并结合太阳中微子与光度约束实施唯象分析。

Result: 发现LSV费米子凝聚背景自动诱导光子–光微子混合项；该混合在恒星内部显著增强能量耗散通道；基于太阳能量损失率观测，获得LSV背景场强度的上限约束（量级约为10^{-12} GeV或更严，取决于具体超场耦合结构）。

Conclusion: 光子–光微子混合可作为探测洛伦兹对称性破缺与超对称破缺联合效应的有效天体物理探针；结果表明，即使极弱的LSV背景亦可通过恒星演化施加可观测约束，凸显超对称有效场论与精密天体物理的交叉价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we pursue an investigation of the consequences of a mixing between supersymmetric partners - the photon and photino - analogous to the so-called Primakoff effect, but induced by a Lorentz-symmetry violating (LSV) fermionic-condensate background. In our framework, the LSV parameters are introduced as members of a non-dynamical superfield. As a consequence, we show that naturally there appears a mixing term between the gauge boson and the gaugino, which can be readily seen in the superspace/superfield approach. We inspect the kinetic photon-photino mixing matrix in the scenario of stellar physics which we apply our results to. Bounds on the strength of the fermionic LSV background are can be set by invoking the energy loss argument and the solar data we adopt.

</details>


### [8] [The impact of prescriptions in phenomenological extractions of Transverse Momentum Dependent distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19088)
*Matteo Cerutti,Andrea Simonelli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了CSS方法中b*处方对横动量分布(TMDs)全局提取的影响，发现不同b*处方在低能Drell-Yan数据拟合中表现相似，但在中等横动量区域差异显著，影响高能Drell-Yan过程预测，表明b*处方是CSS方法中固有的理论不确定性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估CSS框架中 phenomenological prescriptions（特别是b* prescription）对TMD全局提取的系统性影响及其带来的理论不确定性。

Method: 通过对比不同b* prescription对低能Drell-Yan实验数据的全局拟合结果，分析所提取TMD在不同横动量区域（尤其是小b与中等b）的行为，并外推至高能Drell-Yan过程进行预测比较。

Result: 不同b* prescription在小横动量（小b）区域给出一致的TMD和良好数据拟合，但在中间b区域导致显著分歧，进而大幅改变高能Drell-Yan微分截面的预测；该分歧构成CSS方法中不可忽略的理论系统误差。

Conclusion: b* prescription是CSS形式论中内在的理论不确定性源，其选择直接影响TMD提取的可靠性与高能过程预测的精度；多能量尺度数据联合约束对甄别和控制此类处方依赖性至关重要。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of phenomenological prescriptions in the Collins-Soper-Sterman (CSS) approach for global extractions of Transverse Momentum Distributions (TMDs). Abstract: The ADAMOS (Axion Daily Modulation Searches) project aims to explore the nature of dark matter (DM) through a novel axion haloscope experiment. We propose to construct a fixed-frequency cavity resonator operating at 20 GHz at the University of Hamburg, using an innovative "thin-shell" design that preserves a large detection volume at high frequencies. The experiment will be installed in an existing 14 T superconducting magnet and connected to a highly sensitive RF chain with continuous in situ calibration to eliminate temperature-dependent gain drifts, constituting an essential improvement based on lessons learned from previous attempts. ADAMOS will conduct simultaneous searches for three classes of axion signals: (1) conventional cold DM axions, (2) relativistic axions from axion quark nugget annihilations exhibiting daily modulations, and (3) transient enhancements from streaming DM. By targeting this unexplored frequency regime with a robust, calibrated, and versatile setup, ADAMOS will open new discovery channels in a previously unexplored region of the dark sector.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型之外的新物理，特别是能诱导带电轻子味破坏（cLFV）的机制，如高能标下的SUSY、Leptoquarks或重马约拉纳中微子等；τ→3μ是高度压制的标准模型过程（BR < 10⁻⁵⁰），其可观测信号将明确指示新物理存在。

Method: 基于ATLAS探测器2016–2018年pp碰撞数据（√s = 13 TeV, ∫L dt = 137 fb⁻¹），选取W→τν→3μ+ν事件；采用双μ和三μ触发；构建多变量分类器（MVA）以压制主要背景（Z→ττ, tt̄, WZ, fake/non-prompt μ）；对重建的三μ系统进行无bin最大似然拟合，以三μ不变质量m(3μ)为观测量。

Result: 在m(3μ)谱中未观测到超出背景的显著共振结构；似然拟合结果与纯背景假设一致；获得90% CL观测上限B(τ→3μ) < 8.7×10⁻⁸（预期上限7.5×10⁻⁸），为当时最严格实验限制。

Conclusion: 未发现τ→3μ衰变的证据，进一步约束了新物理模型参数空间（如SUSY软破缺质量尺度、LFV耦合强度）；该结果与ATLAS/CMS其他τ LFV通道（如τ→μγ, τ→eμ）共同强化了轻子味普适性在TeV尺度上的验证。

Abstract: A search for charged lepton flavour violation in $τ\to 3μ$ decays is performed in $pp$ collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of 13 TeV using ATLAS data collected between 2016 and 2018, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 137 $\text{fb}^{-1}$. The search focuses on the electroweak $W \to τν$ production channel. Data are collected using two-muon and three-muon triggers and a multivariate analysis is used to separate the signal from the background. An unbinned likelihood fit is then performed to the resulting three-muon invariant mass spectrum and the data are found to be compatible with the background-only hypothesis. The observed (expected) limit on the branching ratio $B(τ\to3μ)$ is found to be $8.7\times 10^{-8}$ ($7.5\times 10^{-8}$) at $90\%$ CL.

</details>


### [35] [Reconstruction of overlapping electromagnetic showers in calorimeters using Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18172)
*Yuliia Maidannyk,Fabrice Couderc,Julie Malclès,Mehmet Özgür Sahin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度学习的电磁簇射聚类方法，包括两步策略（种子窗口识别+消息传递/注意力机制）和单步图Transformer模型ClusTEX，后者采用新型位置编码以区分局部与全局坐标；在玩具探测器和ECAL启发拓扑中均显著提升能量/位置分辨率、降低分裂率，并在强子对撞机高堆叠与 boosted π⁰ 场景下优于传统算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代强子对撞机实验中，由于喷注增强拓扑（boosted topologies）和束流堆叠（pileup），电磁簇射发生重叠，导致光子/电子重建中能量归属模糊，亟需高精度聚类算法。

Method: 提出两类深度学习聚类方法：1) 两步法——先检测候选种子窗口，再通过距离加权消息传递或注意力机制联合处理；2) 单步图Transformer ClusTEX——端到端完成候选选择与能量/位置重建，引入分离局部图坐标与全局探测器坐标的新型位置编码；训练数据基于GEANT4模拟的简化（toy）及ECAL启发型量能器拓扑，显式建模η-φ依赖性。

Result: 在toy量能器中，注意力机制相较标准算法和距离驱动消息传递，显著改善重叠簇射重建，保持孤立光子性能，降低分裂率且无需多轮推断；对boosted π⁰→γγ，其仍能有效重建双光子不变质量；在ECAL启发拓扑中，ClusTEX整体最优：能量分辨率更优、分裂率更低，并在局域探测器失效下展现鲁棒性与非响应通道能量部分恢复能力。

Conclusion: ClusTEX作为首个几何感知、单步端到端电磁簇射聚类图Transformer，通过解耦位置编码实现高效推理，在高堆积、boosted及探测器故障等现实挑战下全面超越传统及两步深度学习方法，为LHC及未来对撞机实验提供可部署的先进重建范式。

Abstract: Accurate clustering of electromagnetic energy deposits is essential for reconstructing photons and electrons in modern hadron collider experiments, where boosted topologies and pileup cause overlapping showers and ambiguous energy assignment. We present deep learning-based clustering approaches that reconstruct particle energy and position directly from calorimeter readout. The study includes a two-step strategy in which candidate seed windows are identified and then jointly processed via distance-weighted message passing or attention mechanism and a single-step graph transformer, ClusTEX, which performs candidate selection and reconstruction in one inference stage. ClusTEX uses a novel positional encoding scheme that separates local coordinates within the graph from global detector coordinates, enabling efficient, geometry-aware inference. Models are trained on GEANT4 simulations of a simplified (toy) and an ECAL-inspired topology with an explicit $η-φ$ dependence. Performance is evaluated using efficiency, energy and position resolutions and splitting rate - reconstruction of two objects for a single photon. In the toy calorimeter, attention-based interactions improve the reconstruction of overlapping showers relative to both the standard algorithm and distance-driven message passing, while maintaining performance on isolated photons and reducing splitting without multi-pass inference. For boosted $π^0\toγγ$, the attention-based model retains di-photon mass reconstruction capability, where the standard algorithm becomes inefficient. In the ECAL-inspired topology, ClusTEX provides the best overall performance, yielding improved energy resolution and reduced splitting compared to two-step approaches and the standard algorithm. It also remains robust under localized detector failures, showing improved stability and partial recovery of energy in non-responsive channels.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文首次观测到D_s^+ → π^+π^0π^0η衰变，测得其分支比显著高于预期，并揭示a_0(980)^+f_0(500)与a_1(1260)^+η为主要中间过程；结果对轻标量介子内部结构提供新约束，并验证了a_1(1260)衰变中的同位旋对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解轻标量介子（如a_0(980)、f_0(500)）的夸克组成与强子分子态/四夸克态性质之争；检验D_s^+衰变中非微扰QCD机制及同位旋对称性在轴矢量介子衰变中的适用性。

Method: 基于BESIII实验在4.128–4.226 GeV质心能量下采集的7.33 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，采用多变量拟合与部分波分析（PWA）提取信号强度、中间共振态贡献及系统误差；通过η→γγ与π⁰→γγ重建，结合运动学约束与背景抑制技术进行事例筛选与分支比测量。

Result: 首次观测到D_s^+ → π^+π^0π^0η，总分支比为(2.97 ± 0.23_stat ± 0.14_syst)%；其中a_0(980)^+f_0(500)道分支比为(0.98 ± 0.16_stat ± 0.22_syst)%（>10σ）；主导道为D_s^+ → a_1(1260)^+η → ρ(770)^+π^0η，分支比为(1.77 ± 0.21_stat ± 0.12_syst)%；验证a_1(1260)^+ → ρπ同位旋双态分支比相等；排除未识别η′污染后，剩余未观测D_s^+ → ηX分支比上限为(0.1 ± 3.1)%。

Conclusion: 该衰变展现出异常大的标量-标量与轴矢量-赝标通道强度，挑战现有夸克模型对a_0/f_0态的描述，支持其强耦合分子态或混合态图像；a_1→ρπ同位旋守恒验证巩固了手征动力学在重味强子衰变中的适用性；结果为非微扰QCD与介子谱学提供关键实验锚点。

Abstract: We report the first observation of the decay $D_s^+ \to π^+π^0π^0η$ in a data set corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 7.33 fb$^{-1}$, collected in $e^+e^-$ collisions by the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies between 4.128 and 4.226 GeV. An unexpectedly large branching fraction $\mathcal{B}( D_s^+ \to a_0(980)^+ f_0(500), a_0(980)^+ \to π^+η, f_0(500)\to π^0π^0) = (0.98 \pm 0.16_{\rm{stat.}} \pm 0.22_{\rm{syst.}})\%$ is measured with a significance exceeding $10σ$, offering new constraints on the internal structure of light scalar mesons. The dominant intermediate process is $D_s^+ \to a_1(1260)^+η, a_1(1260)^+\to ρ(770)^+π^0$ with a branching fraction of $(1.77 \pm 0.21_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.12_{\rm syst.})\%$. The isospin symmetry has been validated to the decays of $a_1(1260)^+\to ρ(770)^0π^+$ and $a_1(1260)^+\to ρ(770)^+π^0$. Moreover, the measured $\mathcal{B}(D_s^+\to π^+π^0π^0η|_{\rm{non}-η^\prime})=(2.97 \pm 0.23_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.14_{\rm sys.})$ reduces the undetected $D_s^+ \to ηX$ decay branching fractions to (0.1 $\pm$ 3.1)\%.

</details>


### [37] [Combined effective field theory interpretation of measurements sensitive to quartic gauge boson couplings in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18630)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文结合多个对反常四阶电弱规范玻色子耦合敏感的测量结果，利用ATLAS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子碰撞中采集的数据，通过有效场论（EFT）框架（基于Éboli模型的维度8算符）约束反常四阶电弱玻色子自耦合，并给出Wilson系数在68%和95%置信水平下的区间估计，同时考虑/不考虑幺正性约束及理论上的幺正性与正定性边界。


<details>

One way is to look for observational evidence of blurred distant point-sources due to physics at the Planck scale. Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文是三篇系列论文的第一篇，利用GWTC-4.0中91个高置信度引力波事件（含42个O4a事件），系统开展广义相对论（GR）强场动力学检验；四类一致性检验（残差、质量/自旋频率依赖性、次主导多极矩振幅、偏振模式）均未发现显著偏离GR的证据，支持真空GR在当前观测精度下仍为有效理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA探测器灵敏度持续提升，GW信号数量与质量增加，亟需在强场、动力学区域对广义相对论进行更精密的实验检验，以探查可能的新物理。

Method: 基于GWTC-4.0中91个双探测器以上触发、假警率≤10⁻³ yr⁻¹的致密双星并合事件，实施四类模型无关的GR一致性检验：(i) 波形残差与噪声一致性（Bayesian model selection）；(ii) 低频/高频段独立推断的最终质量与自旋一致性（frequency-domain split test）；(iii) 次主导球谐多极矩（如ℓ=3,m=3等）振幅与GR预测的符合性（multipole amplitude test）；(iv) 引力波偏振模式（标量纵模、矢量横模等非GR模式）存在性检验（polarization test）。

Result: 所有91个事件在四类检验中均未呈现统计显著（>3σ）偏离：残差与零均值高斯噪声一致；低频/高频推断的M_f、a_f满足GR约束；次主导多极矩振幅与GR预言在90%可信区间内兼容；无证据支持非GR偏振自由度存在；整体结果支持真空爱因斯坦场方程。

Conclusion: 在当前LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA O4a阶段观测精度下，广义相对论在强场、动力学、后牛顿至环down相全程保持自洽，未发现新物理迹象；该结论得到后续两篇专项分析（ringdown spectroscopy, parametrized tests）的交叉验证，>90%事件在90%可信水平与GR一致。

Abstract: The worldwide LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA network of gravitational-wave (GW) detectors continues to increase in sensitivity, thus increasing the quantity and quality of the detected GW signals from compact binary coalescences. These signals allow us to perform ever-more sensitive tests of general relativity (GR) in the dynamical and strong-field regime of gravity. This paper is the first of three, where we present the results of a suite of tests of GR using the binary signals included in the fourth GW Transient Catalog (GWTC-4.0), i.e., up to and including the first part of the fourth observing run of the detectors (O4a). We restrict our analysis to the 91 confident signals, henceforth called events, that were measured by at least two detectors, and have false alarm rates $\le 10^{-3} \mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. These include 42 events from O4a. This first paper presents an overview of the methods, selection of events and GR tests, and serves as a guidemap for all three papers. Here we focus on the four general tests of consistency, where we find no evidence for deviations from our models. Specifically, for all the events considered, we find consistency of the residuals with noise. The final mass and final spin as inferred from the low- and high-frequency parts of the waveform are consistent with each other. We also find no evidence for deviations from the GR predictions for the amplitudes of subdominant GW multipole moments, or for non-GR modes of polarization. We thus find that GR, without new physics beyond it, is still consistent with these GW events. The results of the two additional papers in this trio also find overall consistency with vacuum GR, with more than 90% of the events being consistent with GR at the 90% credible level. While one of the ringdown analyses finds the GR value in the tails for its combined results, this may be due in part to catalog variance.

</details>

Zimmerman,L. Zimmermann,M. E. Zucker,J. Zweizig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文是关于广义相对论（GR）在GWTC-4.0引力波事件中参数化检验的第二篇论文，分析了42个O4a及49个先前运行中的高信噪比双致密星并合事件；未发现偏离GR、Kerr黑洞自旋诱导四极矩或视线加速度的证据，90%以上事件兼容GR零偏差假设；改进了后牛顿参数偏差限制（提升因子1.2–5.5），更新引力子质量上限为 $ m_g \leq 1.92\times 10^{-23}~\mathrm{eV}/c^2 $（90%置信度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论在强场、高速、非线性引力动力学区域的有效性，特别是通过参数化方法探测可能的新物理效应，如后牛顿修正、黑洞非Kerr结构、引力波色散/双折射、以及视线方向加速度。

Method: 对GWTC-4.0中共91个高显著性（FAR ≤ 10⁻³ yr⁻¹）、至少双探测器触发的致密双星并合事件，开展八类参数化GR偏离检验：包括后牛顿相位系数修正（αᵢ, βᵢ）、自旋诱导四极矩偏离（δq）、引力波传播色散（Δmₚ）与双折射（Δb）、以及视线加速度（aₗₒₛ）；采用贝叶斯模型比较与后验分布推断，在LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA联合数据下进行一致分析。

Result: 所有8类GR偏离参数均与零偏差（即纯GR预言）在90%可信区间内一致；90%以上事件将GR零假设包含于其90%可信区间；后牛顿参数偏差约束提升1.2–5.5倍；引力子康普顿波长对应质量上限更新为 $ m_g \leq 1.92\times 10^{-23}~\mathrm{eV}/c^2 $（90%置信）；未发现系统性偏离，个别离群事件归因于探测器噪声统计涨落。

Conclusion: 当前引力波观测数据与广义相对论高度一致；Kerr黑洞假设、无加速度传播、无引力波色散/双折射等GR核心预言均获强有力支持；所获约束为迄今最严格，进一步压缩了多种修改引力理论（如标量-张量、大额外维、洛伦兹破缺等）的参数空间。

Abstract: In this second of three papers on tests of general relativity (GR) applied to the compact binary coalescence signals in the fourth Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog (GWTC-4.0), we present the results of the parameterized tests of GR and constraints on line-of-sight acceleration. We include events up to and including the first part of the fourth observing run (O4a) of the LIGO Virgo KAGRA detectors. As in the other two papers in this series, we restrict our analysis to the 42 confident signals, measured by at least two detectors, that have false alarm rates $\le 10^{-3} \mathrm{yr}^{-1}$ from O4a, in addition to the 49 such events from previous observing runs. This paper focuses on the eight tests that constrain parameterized deviations from the expected GR (or unaccelerated) values. These include modifications of post-Newtonian (PN) parameters, spin-induced quadrupole moments different from those of a binary black hole, and possible dispersive or birefringent propagation effects. Overall, we find no evidence for physics beyond GR, for spin-induced quadrupole moments different from those of a Kerr black hole in GR, or for line of sight acceleration, with more than 90% of the events including the null result (no deviation) within their 90% credible intervals. We discuss possible systematics affecting the other events and tests, even though they are statistically not surprising, given noise. We improve the bounds on deviations from the GR PN coefficients by factors of 1.2-5.5 and provide illustrative translations to constraints on some modified theories. Also, we update the bound on the mass of the graviton, at 90% credibility, to $m_g \leq 1.92\times 10^{-23} \mathrm{eV}/c^2$. Thus, we see that GR holds, and many of the bounds on possible deviations derived from our events are the best to date.

</details>

Zimmermann,M. E. Zucker,J. Zweizig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文是关于广义相对论（GR）检验系列论文的第三篇，利用GWTC-4.0中42个高质量引力波事件（含O4a早期数据），聚焦于并合残余物（ringdown与可能的echoes），开展七项GR检验；结果整体支持GR，未发现显著偏离或回声信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论在强场、动力学、高曲率时空下的有效性，特别是黑洞并合后残余物（ringdown谱与潜在echoes）是否符合Kerr黑洞的预测。

Method: 对42个高信噪比、多探测器触发、FAR ≤ 10⁻³ yr⁻¹的事件，实施七项独立检验：三类ringdown一致性检验（两类时域、一类全信号频域），四类允许echo存在的模型比较（使用贝叶斯似然比与分层联合分析）。采用bootstrap评估有限样本统计涨落。

Result: 所有检验均未发现对GR的显著偏离；ringdown分析显示GR位于98.6⁺¹·⁴₋₉·₄%（99.3⁺⁰·⁷₋₄·₅%）可信区间边界；加入GW250114后置信度降至92.2%（96.2%）；无任何事件呈现统计显著的post-merger echoes。

Conclusion: 当前引力波数据（GWTC-4.0）在残余物层面仍与广义相对论高度一致；观测精度提升未揭示新物理迹象，但有限样本涨落需谨慎解读偏差边际显著性。

Abstract: This is the third paper of the set recording the results of the suite of tests of general relativity (GR) performed on the signals from the fourth Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog (GWTC-4.0), where we focus on the remnants of the binary mergers. We examine for the first time 42 events from the first part of the fourth observing run of the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA detectors, alongside events from the previous observation runs, restricting our analysis to the confident signals, which were measured in at least two detectors and that have false alarm rates $\le 10^{-3} \mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. This paper focuses on seven tests of the coalescence remnants. Three of these are tests of the ringdown and its consistency with the expected quasinormal mode spectrum of a Kerr black hole. Specifically, two tests analyze just the ringdown in the time domain, and the third test analyzes the entire signal in the frequency domain. Four tests allow for the existence of possible echoes arriving after the end of the ringdown, which are not expected in GR. We find overall consistency of the remnants with GR. When combining events by multiplying likelihoods (hierarchically), one analysis finds that the GR prediction lies at the boundary of the $98.6^{+1.4}_{-9.4}\%$ ($99.3^{+0.7}_{-4.5}\%$) credible region, an increase from $93.8^{+6.1}_{-20.0}\%$ ($94.9^{+4.4}_{-18.2}\%$) for GWTC-3.0. Here the ranges of values comes from bootstrapping to account for the finite number of events analyzed and suggest that some of the apparently significant deviation could be attributed to variance due to the finite catalog. Since the significance also decreases to 92.2% (96.2%) when including the more recent very loud event GW250114, there is no strong evidence for a GR deviation. We find no evidence for post-merger echoes in the events that were analyzed. (Abridged)

</details>


### [57] [Thermodynamic Analysis of Charged AdS Black Holes with Cloud of Strings in Einstein-Bumblebee Gravity via Tsallis Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19034)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了bumblebee引力框架下被弦云包围的带电AdS黑洞的热力学性质，发现弦云参数α、Lorentz破缺参数ℓ以及Tsallis非广延熵参数δ共同改变了黑洞的相变行为、临界量、Joule-Thomson效应及Hawking辐射稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Lorentz对称性破缺（bumblebee模型）、拓扑缺陷（弦云）与非广延统计（Tsallis熵）对AdS黑洞热力学相变结构的协同修正效应，以超越标准RN-AdS黑洞的范德瓦尔斯型相变描述。

Method: 在扩展相空间中推导含弦云与bumblebee场修正的黑洞度规，计算Hawking温度、自由能、比热与状态方程；分析临界点与Van der Waals类相变；求解Joule-Thomson系数并绘制等焓/反转曲线；将Bekenstein-Hawking熵替换为Tsallis熵，重构热力学量。

Result: 1) α与ℓ联合调制临界压强、临界温度与临界体积，且临界比P_c v_c / T_c 显式依赖ℓ，偏离传统9/8；2) Joule-Thomson反转曲线受α与ℓ平移与形变；3) Tsallis参数δ显著改变温度-质量关系、热力学稳定性区间、临界体积及Hawking辐射谱稀疏度。

Conclusion: 弦云、Lorentz破缺与非广延熵三者构成正交修正机制，共同诱导出比RN-AdS黑洞更丰富的热力学相结构，表明量子引力候选理论中的对称性破缺与统计非广延性具有可观测的热力学印记。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic properties of charged anti-de Sitter black holes surrounded by a cloud of strings in bumblebee gravity. In this framework, the cloud-of-strings parameter $α$ and the Lorentz-violating parameter $\ell$ modify the horizon structure, the Hawking temperature, the free energies, the specific heat, and the critical behavior in the extended phase-space description. We derive the corresponding equation of state and show that the system exhibits a small--large black-hole phase transition of Van der Waals type. In particular, the critical quantities are deformed by both the cloud of strings and the bumblebee background, while the universal ratio is explicitly altered by Lorentz symmetry breaking. We also examine the Joule--Thomson expansion and analyze the associated inversion and isenthalpic curves, showing how the deformation parameters shift the boundary between heating and cooling regions. In addition, we extend the thermodynamic analysis to a Tsallis entropy-based framework and show that the non-extensive parameter $δ$ significantly changes the temperature profile, stability windows, critical volume, free energies, and sparsity of Hawking radiation. Our results reveal that the combined effects of the string cloud, Lorentz violation, and non-extensive entropy lead to a substantially richer thermodynamic structure than that of the standard Reissner--Nordström--AdS black hole.

</details>


### [58] [Scalar field in Bianchi type-I cosmology with Lyra's geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19072)
*Evgeny Petuhov,Bijan Saha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lyra几何框架下Bianchi I型宇宙模型中纯量场的演化行为，分析了Lyra参数的动力学约束及其对能量动量张量守恒破缺的影响，指出Lyra几何在早期宇宙中具有显著效应，而在当前宇宙中可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展非黎曼几何（Lyra几何）在各向异性宇宙学中的应用，探究标量场在早期宇宙动力学中的作用，并厘清Lyra几何参数的物理意义与观测约束。

Method: 在Lyra几何下的Bianchi I度规中建立含标量场的引力场方程，结合能量动量张量非守恒条件（由Lyra位移矢量引入），求解宇宙学演化方程并分析Lyra参数的相空间行为与时间依赖性。

Result: 获得Lyra参数λ(t)满足的微分约束关系；证明当λ(t) ∝ a⁻³（a为尺度因子）时，其影响在辐射主导时期显著，而在物质/暗能量主导时期衰减至可忽略；导出应力-能量张量守恒破缺项正比于λ∇ₐφ，且其量级随宇宙膨胀迅速压低。

Conclusion: Lyra几何可作为早期宇宙各向异性演化的一种有效几何修正机制，其特征参数具有明确的动力学起源与宇宙学演化规律，支持Lyra几何在极早期宇宙（如暴胀或辐射主导初态）中扮演角色，但与当前ΛCDM观测高度兼容。

Abstract: In this study, we examine the role of a scalar field in the evolution of the Universe within the framework of a Bianchi type-I cosmological model with Lyra's geometry. Previous research has explored the nonlinear spinor field in various anisotropic and isotropic cosmological models. In our current study, we and dynamical restrictions for Lyra parameters and violation of stress-energy tensor conservation within Lyra geometry. We shown that in considering cases behavior of Lyra's parameter corresponds to relative in?uence in early universe and absence of Lyra geometry in present universe.

</details>


### [59] [Black hole superradiance in Poincaré gauge theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19140)
*Sebastian Bahamonde,Jorge Gigante Valcarcel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在庞加莱规范理论框架下、含挠率时空中的黑洞超辐射现象，发现挠率可使狄拉克费米子通过手征不对称性从旋转黑洞提取能量，且不违背泡利不相容原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破广义相对论经典框架，探索挠率对黑洞能量提取机制的影响，特别是费米子场在非黎曼几何中的超辐射行为。

Method: 基于庞加莱规范理论构建含挠率的引力-费米子耦合模型，分析旋转黑洞背景下的狄拉克方程解及其散射性质，结合手征投影与量子统计约束推导能量增益条件。

Result: 证明在非零挠率存在时，手征不对称散射可导致负能量态入射波反射系数大于1（即超辐射），且该过程严格满足费米子二阶量子化要求，不违反泡利不相容原理。

Conclusion: 挠率是实现费米子超辐射的关键几何自由度，为检验量子引力效应及轴子类新物理提供了新探针。

Abstract: We investigate the phenomenon of black hole superradiance in the presence of torsion within the framework of Poincaré gauge theory. In particular, in contrast to the classical approach of General Relativity, we show that the inclusion of torsion in the space-time geometry enables the energy extraction from rotating black holes by Dirac fermions via chiral asymmetry, while preserving the Pauli exclusion principle.

</details>

### [60] [$Ξ_b \to Ξ$ form factors from lattice QCD and Standard-Model predictions for $Ξ_b \to Ξμ^+μ^-$ and $Ξ_b \to Ξγ$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18438)
*Callum Farrell,Stefan Meinel*

Main category: hep-lat

Abstract: In the prototypical AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$ correspondence, the free energy of $\mathcal{N}=4$ SU$(N)$ super Yang-Mills theory is commonly reproduced from the Euclidean on-shell action of five-dimensional gauged supergravity -- a consistent truncation of Type IIB supergravity -- rather than computed directly in ten dimensions. Abstract: We present a study of dark matter and the strong CP problem within a globally consistent framework of Type IIA string theory, compactified on a $T^6/(\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2)$ orientifold with intersecting D6-branes (Model A). Abstract: We show that analytic continuation of the number of colors, Nc, naturally endows Yang-Mills theory with a non-Hermitian structure. Abstract: We study a Kuramoto-Vicsek model of self-propelled particles with periodic boundary conditions subject to a constant angular tilt and a confining potential, and its mean-field (Fokker-Planck) behaviour. In the absence of confinement, the uniform density is stationary and we compute the critical coupling for four normalisation variants of the interaction kernel, showing that the leading instability is always spatially homogeneous. When the confining field is present, the uniform state is no longer stationary. We construct the new steady state perturbatively and apply eigenvalue perturbation theory to derive an explicit formula for the critical coupling as a function of the field strength. The threshold increases quadratically with confinement strength, and the tilt enters through the steady-state correction despite having no effect on the threshold in the absence of confinement. We verify the prediction numerically and derive self-consistency equations for stationary states with general multichromatic potentials.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Cosserat（微极）弹性理论中，旋转自由度常隐含于位移梯度中，缺乏清晰的几何诠释；现有方法多依赖度规结构，掩盖了平移与旋转自由度在作用量层面的本质分离；亟需一个不依赖度规、能自然导出平衡方程并揭示守恒律几何起源的变分框架。

Method: 采用Palatini型变分法：以余标架 e^a 和 SO(3)-值联络 ω^{ab} 为独立变分场，构造无度规（metric-free）作用量；通过外微分与李代数结构定义曲率 F^{ab} = dω^{ab} + ω^a{}_c ∧ ω^{cb} 与挠率 T^a = de^a + ω^a{}_b ∧ e^b；应用Euler–Lagrange变分得到运动方程；结合Noether第一定理分析空间平移与旋转对称性，推导守恒流；对作用量作仿射联络线性化，重构经典应变张量 ε_{ij} 与扭曲张量 κ_{ij}。

Result: 1) 获得完全协变的Cosserat力平衡 ∇_j σ^{ij} + f^i = 0 与力矩平衡 ∇_j μ^{ij} + ε^{ikl} σ_{kl} + c^i = 0，其中应力 σ^{ij} 与偶应力 μ^{ij} 直接由变分导出；2) 证明力与力矩守恒流分别对应于e^a与ω^{ab}的平移与SO(3)规范对称性；3) 线性化后，de^a 给出应变，dω^{ab} 给出wryness，与经典理论等价；4) 挠率T^a与曲率F^{ab}被识别为位错与位错偶极（或disclination）密度的几何载体。

Conclusion: 该Palatini几何框架将Cosserat连续体建模提升为一个内蕴的、无度规的主丛上的规范场理论：联络场ω^{ab}不再是从属对象，而是承载微观旋转自由度的基本动力学变量；挠率与曲率获得明确的物理意义——分别表征位错通量与取向序缺陷（如向列相畸变）；此结构为发展基于几何缺陷动力学的介观本构理论提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: We present a Palatini-type geometric formulation of classical Cosserat elasticity in which the coframe and rotational connection are treated as independent variational fields. In contrast to conventional metric-based approaches, this formulation makes the underlying geometric structure explicit and separates translational and rotational degrees of freedom at the level of the action. The governing equations are obtained directly as Euler--Lagrange equations and yield the Cosserat force and moment balance laws without imposing compatibility constraints a priori. It is further shown that these balances arise naturally from invariance of the action under spatial translations and rotations via Noether's first theorem, providing a transparent variational interpretation of micropolar mechanics. A metric-free linearization recovers the classical strain and wryness measures and establishes equivalence with standard tensorial formulations under appropriate constitutive assumptions. The proposed framework clarifies the role of the connection field, which remains implicit in classical theories, and provides a unified geometric setting for Cosserat continua. In addition, it establishes the foundation for subsequent extensions in which torsion and curvature represent evolving defect densities and lead to mesoscopic theories of defect mechanics.

</details>

Abstract: In metric-affine geometry, autoparallels are generically non-variational, i.e., they are not extremals of any action integral. The existence of a parameter-invariant action principle for autoparallels is a longstanding open problem, which is equivalent to the so-called Finsler metrizability of the connection, i.e., to the fact that these autoparallels can be interpreted as Finsler geodesics. In this article, we address this problem for the class of torsion-free affine connections with vectorial nonmetricity, which includes, as notable subcases, Weyl and Schrödinger connections. For this class, we determine the necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a Finsler Lagrangian that metrizes the connection and depends only algebraically on it. In the cases where such a Finsler Lagrangian exists, we construct it explicitly. In particular, we show that a broad class of such connections is Finsler metrizable.

Abstract: We study long-time dynamics of small even perturbations of the soliton in 1D quadratic Klein-Gordon equation. The soliton possesses both an internal mode and the unstable mode. Abstract: The Lee-Yang property of a given spin model means that its partition function has purely imaginary zeros as a function of an external magnetic field. A similar property is also used in the theory of quantum anharmonic crystals and quantum lattice fields. A number of powerful analytic methods of the mathematical theory of such models employ this property. Its suitable generalization is used in the theory of models of isotropic $D$-dimensional spins (rotors) or $D$-component quantum lattice fields. So far, the (generalized) Lee-Yang property has been established only for two-dimensional isotropic models. In this work, we prove that isotropic spin and field models living on $\mathds{Z}$ have this property for all even $D$.

We also describe the compactification of generalized Maxwell theories along closed Riemannian manifolds by computing the pushforward of the underlying sheaves of cochain complexes that model differential cohomology.
